Es pequeño, pero importante para robustez.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Resultado esperado

Con esos cambios, esta cadena debería funcionar de verdad:

* evento MIDI o trigger UI actualiza `mGlobalMidiToCv`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* el bloque `implementationId 501` publica pitch/gate/velocity en `modSignals[60..62]`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `ModulationRegistry` resuelve `mcv.1.pitch/gate/vel`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `ModulationMatrixService` traduce `A.pitch/A.gate/A.gain` a IDs runtime de `pitch/gate/gain`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `EngineVoiceRuntime` consume esos IDs en oscilador, ADSR y VCA.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

En otras palabras: con este parche,  **tu preset actual deja de ser decorativo y pasa a ser operativo** .[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Lo único que dejaría fuera

Seguiría dejando fuera por ahora `trig.1.midi -> mcv.1.midi` como conexión de eventos real dentro de la modMatrix. Esa parte debería vivir en otro tipo de routing, porque hoy `RuntimeModRoute` representa valores CV/param, no streams MIDI.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

De momento, si el trigger ya inyecta a MIDI global, te basta para validar el flujo sonoro completo. Luego, cuando quieras, se diseña una segunda familia de rutas tipo `EventRoute` o `MidiRoute` sin contaminar la mod matrix CV.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Si quieres, el siguiente paso útil es que te proponga **una mini batería de tests/manual checks** para verificar que después del parche realmente cambian `pitch`, `gate` y `gain` en tiempo real.




Sí. Te propongo una batería de verificación  **muy corta pero muy fiable** , pensada para confirmar que el parche realmente cerró la ruta `UI Trigger/MIDI -> MCV -> mod matrix -> pitch/gate/gain` y no solo “compila”. El bundle confirma que el botón de trigger de la UI llama a `RpcInputController::handleTriggerNote()`, que a su vez invoca `OmegaAudioProcessor::triggerNote()` y mete `noteOn/noteOff` en `mUiMidiQueue`, luego inyectada en `processBlock()` junto al MIDI hardware.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Prueba 1: Gate

La prueba más básica es comprobar que `A.gate` ya abre y cierra envolvente de verdad. En tu runtime, el ADSR lee `gate` mediante `getModSignal(idGate, ...)` y ejecuta `AceUnitAdsr::setGate(..., gateSignal > 0.5f)`, así que si el mapping quedó bien, el botón FIRE debe producir un ataque/release claro y repetible.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Haz esto:

* deja solo una ruta activa: `mcv.1.gate -> A.gate`, amount 1.0;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* usa un patch muy simple con oscilador fijo y VCA/ADSR audibles;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* pulsa FIRE y suelta.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Resultado esperado:

* al pulsar, aparece sonido;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* al soltar, entra release;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* si desconectas esa ruta, el comportamiento debe desaparecer o cambiar claramente.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Si no cambia nada, el problema sigue en `Gate` target resolution o en la traducción a `ParamIdRegistry("gate")`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Prueba 2: Pitch

El oscilador consulta `idPitch` y suma `pitchCv` a `state.frequencyHz` antes de procesar la voz, mientras que el módulo MIDI→CV publica `lastPitchHz` en la salida de pitch del MCV. Eso significa que, con `mcv.1.pitch -> A.pitch`, deberías percibir cambio de nota al disparar distintas teclas o distintas selecciones de nota en el trigger UI.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Haz esto:

* deja activas `mcv.1.pitch -> A.pitch` y `mcv.1.gate -> A.gate`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* en el trigger UI cambia la nota u octava varias veces;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* dispara varias notas separadas.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Resultado esperado:

* cada disparo debe salir con altura distinta;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* si todas suenan con la misma afinación, el gate está entrando pero el pitch no está llegando;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* si suenan desafinadas de forma absurda, probablemente estés sumando Hz a un parámetro que realmente esperaba semitonos o viceversa.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Ese último punto es importante: el runtime actual publica `lastPitchHz`, no semitonos, así que esta prueba también te dirá si tu contrato de pitch está bien definido o si has mezclado dominios físicos distintos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Prueba 3: Velocity a gain

El MCV publica `lastVelocity` y el VCA usa `gain` como parámetro modulable. Por tanto, con `mcv.1.vel -> A.gain`, diferentes velocidades deberían traducirse en distintas intensidades de salida.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Haz esto:

* deja activa la ruta `mcv.1.vel -> A.gain`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* dispara la misma nota varias veces con velocities distintas, por ejemplo 30, 80 y 127;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* mantén pitch y gate conectados para que la voz sea audible.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Resultado esperado:

* la nota debe salir más floja o más fuerte según la velocidad;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* si todo suena igual, revisa que `mcv.1.vel` esté resolviendo a `CompiledSignalSpace::kMidiToCvVelocity` y que `A.gain` acabe en `ParamIdRegistry("gain")`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Prueba 4: Confirmación MIDI UI

Como el trigger UI empuja mensajes a `mUiMidiQueue` y `processBlock()` los mezcla con el MIDI real antes del adaptador de entrada, una forma útil de aislar fallos es verificar que la UI realmente está metiendo MIDI en el sistema. Además, `RpcInputController` también registra eventos en `MidiMonitor`, y el `ModuleMidiViewer` consulta esos eventos por telemetría/monitoring.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Haz esto:

* abre el módulo MIDI Viewer / Midi Probe;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* pulsa FIRE varias veces desde la UI;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* mira si aparecen Note On / Note Off coherentes.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Resultado esperado:

* deben verse los eventos aunque el motor aún no suene perfecto;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* si no aparecen, el problema está antes del engine, en RPC/UI queue;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* si aparecen pero no suena, el fallo está en MCV/mod matrix/runtime.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Prueba 5: Aislar con matrix mínima

Como `ModulationMatrixService::compileMatrix()` resetea `outPlan.modRouteCount` y vuelve a poblar rutas desde los slots activos, conviene hacer una prueba con la matriz mínima posible para evitar contaminación de rutas viejas o defaults confusos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Usa solo estos tres slots:

* `mcv.1.pitch -> A.pitch`, amount 1.0;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `mcv.1.gate -> A.gate`, amount 1.0;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `mcv.1.vel -> A.gain`, amount 1.0.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Y desactiva el resto. Si con esa configuración mínima funciona, entonces el core del pipeline está cerrado y cualquier fallo posterior ya será de diseño musical, amounts o conflictos de rutas, no de plumbing.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Prueba 6: Telemetría útil

Tu plugin ya empuja señales a `ModulationTelemetryHub` y la UI dispone de módulos tipo scope/probe y MIDI probe alimentados por telemetría e inventario semántico. Aunque no he visto en el bundle que `modSignals[60..62]` se publiquen directamente a telemetría de UI, sí tienes la infraestructura necesaria para añadir logs o probes de forma muy localizada si necesitas confirmar valores numéricos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

La comprobación más útil aquí sería temporal:

* loggear una vez por bloque los valores de `state.modSignals[kMidiToCvPitch/Gate/Velocity]`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* o exponerlos provisionalmente como señales de probe;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y verificar que cambian al pulsar FIRE o al cambiar velocidad/nota.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Eso te diría enseguida si el MCV publica bien pero la matrix no enruta, o al revés.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Fallos típicos que esperaría

Hay tres fallos muy probables:

* `A.gate` resuelve, pero `targetParamId` no coincide con `ParamIdRegistry("gate")`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `A.pitch` llega, pero el valor está en Hz y el oscilador espera otra unidad;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `A.gain` funciona, pero la base del VCA ya está tan alta o tan normalizada que apenas percibes la diferencia de velocity.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

O sea, si gate funciona primero, eso ya es una victoria importante. Luego pitch y velocity se afinan como contrato de unidades y escala.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Orden recomendado

Yo haría las pruebas en este orden:

1. Ver que FIRE genera Note On/Off en MIDI Viewer.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
2. Ver que `mcv.1.gate -> A.gate` abre/cierra sonido.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
3. Ver que `mcv.1.pitch -> A.pitch` cambia la nota.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
4. Ver que `mcv.1.vel -> A.gain` cambia la intensidad.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Ese orden te aísla muy bien dónde se rompe la tubería.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Si quieres, el siguiente paso te lo puedo dejar todavía más práctico: te doy una **checklist de debug con exactamente qué `DBG()` o logs poner y en qué funciones** para localizar el fallo en menos de 10 minutos.



Sí. Te dejo una checklist de debug  **muy quirúrgica** , con logs concretos y en el orden correcto para que localices el fallo en minutos. El bundle ya muestra que tienes `DBG()` y logging en varias zonas del proyecto, que `OmegaAudioProcessor::triggerNote()` mete MIDI en `mUiMidiQueue`, que `processBlock()` mezcla ese MIDI con el hardware y que `ModulationMatrixService::compileMatrix()` recompila `outPlan.modRoutes` desde el preset, así que el mejor enfoque es seguir la señal punto por punto.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 1) Confirmar entrada UI -> MIDI

Primero confirma que el botón FIRE realmente genera Note On/Off y entra en el audio thread. `RpcInputController::handleTriggerNote()` llama a `OmegaAudioProcessor::triggerNote()`, y luego `processBlock()` vuelca `mUiMidiQueue` dentro de `midiMessages`, así que aquí solo necesitas un log en ambos extremos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[UI-MIDI] queue -> processBlock events="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> mUiMidiQueue</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">getNumEvents</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>            midiMessages</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">addEvents</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>mUiMidiQueue</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> buffer</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">getNumSamples</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>            mUiMidiQueue</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">clear</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token operator">!</span><span>midiMessages</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">isEmpty</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[MIDI] merged events in block="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> midiMessages</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">getNumEvents</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Si esto no aparece, el fallo está antes del engine.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 2) Confirmar que el preset compila rutas válidas

Tu `EngineConfigManager::applyPreset()` compila la voice architecture y luego llama a `ModulationMatrixService::compileMatrix(preset, compileResult.plan)`, así que este es el sitio clave para ver si `mcv.1.pitch`, `A.pitch`, etc. se resuelven o se descartan.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Lo que quieres ver es exactamente:

* `mcv.1.pitch -> idx 60`,[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `mcv.1.gate -> idx 61`,[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `mcv.1.vel -> idx 62`,[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y `A.pitch/A.gate/A.gain` convertidos a runtime IDs no nulos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 3) Confirmar que el plan llega al engine

Aunque compile bien, conviene confirmar que el `CompiledVoicePlan` resultante sí se entrega al engine. `EngineConfigManager::applyPreset()` llama a `mEngine.setVoicePlan(compileResult.plan)` cuando `VoiceArchitectureCompiler::compile()` tiene éxito.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Pon un log ahí:

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// Core/Service/EngineConfigManager.h/.cpp</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>success</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">ModulationMatrixService</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">compileMatrix</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>preset</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[ENGINE-CONFIG] voice plan compiled:"</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" units="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>unitCount
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" conns="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>connectionCount
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" modRoutes="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modRouteCount</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>    mEngine</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">setVoicePlan</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">else</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[ENGINE-CONFIG] voice plan compile FAILED: "</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> compileResult</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>errorMessage</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Si aquí ves `modRoutes=0`, el problema está antes del DSP.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 4) Confirmar MCV en runtime

Luego toca comprobar que el bloque `implementationId 501` realmente publica valores útiles en `modSignals`. En `EngineVoiceRuntime::renderSample()` el caso 501 escribe pitch/gate/velocity en `state.modSignals[60..62]`, así que mete un log solo para `voiceIdx == 0` y solo ocasionalmente para no inundar.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// Core/Voice/EngineVoiceRuntime.h</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">case</span><span></span><span class="token token">501</span><span class="token token operator">:</span><span></span><span class="token token">// MIDI to CV Utility</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>    state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvPitch</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> pools</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>lastPitchHz</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>    state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvGate</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> pools</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>isGateActive </span><span class="token token operator">?</span><span></span><span class="token token">1.0f</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">:</span><span></span><span class="token token">0.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>    state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvVelocity</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> pools</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>lastVelocity</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>voiceIdx </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">static</span><span></span><span class="token token">int</span><span> dbgCounter </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>dbgCounter</span><span class="token token operator">++</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">%</span><span></span><span class="token token">128</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[MCV] pitch="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvPitch</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" gate="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvGate</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" vel="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvVelocity</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>    unitOut </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>CompiledSignalSpace</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>kMidiToCvGate</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">break</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Si pulsas FIRE y `gate` nunca pasa a 1.0, el problema está en `mGlobalMidiToCv` o en la vía MIDI previa.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 5) Confirmar que la ruta se consume

Ahora toca ver si el runtime usa esas rutas. `EngineVoiceRuntime` calcula `getModSignal(idPitch, ...)` y `getModSignal(idGate, ...)`, y el VCA usa `getModulatedParam()` con `gain`, así que ahí debes ver si las rutas del plan coinciden con los IDs que el DSP espera.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Añade este helper temporal:

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// Dentro de EngineVoiceRuntime::renderSample(), tras definir idGate/idPitch/idSync</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>voiceIdx </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">static</span><span></span><span class="token token">int</span><span> dbgRoutes </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>dbgRoutes</span><span class="token token operator">++</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">%</span><span></span><span class="token token">256</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[ROUTES] plan.modRouteCount="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modRouteCount</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">for</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">int</span><span> m </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span> m </span><span class="token token operator"><</span><span> plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modRouteCount</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">++</span><span>m</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">const</span><span></span><span class="token token">auto</span><span class="token token operator">&</span><span> r </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> plan</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modRoutes</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>m</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[ROUTES] #"</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> m
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" src="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">int</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>r</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>sourceSignal
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" target="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> r</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>targetParamId
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" amount="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> r</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>amount</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[ROUTES] idPitch="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> idPitch
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" idGate="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> idGate
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" idGain="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">ParamIdRegistry</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">getInstance</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">getStableId</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"gain"</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Y dentro de los casos 101, 301 y 401:

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">case</span><span></span><span class="token token">101</span><span class="token token operator">:</span><span></span><span class="token token">// Juno DCO</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> pitchCv </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">getModSignal</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>idPitch</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span></span><span class="token token">0.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> freq </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>frequencyHz </span><span class="token token operator">+</span><span> pitchCv</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>voiceIdx </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">static</span><span></span><span class="token token">int</span><span> dbgOsc </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>dbgOsc</span><span class="token token operator">++</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">%</span><span></span><span class="token token">256</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[OSC] baseHz="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>frequencyHz </span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" pitchCv="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> pitchCv </span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" freq="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> freq</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>    unitOut </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> pools</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>junoOsc</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">process</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>voiceIdx</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> freq</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> lfoVal</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">break</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">case</span><span></span><span class="token token">401</span><span class="token token operator">:</span><span></span><span class="token token">// ADSR</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">static</span><span></span><span class="token token">int</span><span> dbgAdsr </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>dbgAdsr</span><span class="token token operator">++</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">%</span><span></span><span class="token token">256</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[ADSR] gateSignal="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> gateSignal</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">break</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">case</span><span></span><span class="token token">301</span><span class="token token operator">:</span><span></span><span class="token token">// VCA</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> gainCv </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">getModulatedParam</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span></span><span class="token token">"gain"</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>voiceIdx </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">static</span><span></span><span class="token token">int</span><span> dbgVca </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>dbgVca</span><span class="token token operator">++</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">%</span><span></span><span class="token token">256</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">DBG</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"[VCA] input="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> inputSum </span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" gainCv="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> gainCv </span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" out="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>inputSum </span><span class="token token operator">*</span><span> gainCv</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>    unitOut </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> inputSum </span><span class="token token operator">*</span><span> gainCv</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">break</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Eso te dirá inmediatamente si:

* hay rutas en el plan pero no coinciden con `idPitch/idGate/idGain`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* o coinciden pero el source signal vale 0;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* o ambos están bien y el problema ya es de unidades o escalado.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 6) Confirmar IDs de `ParamIdRegistry`

Como uno de los riesgos era mezclar `TargetStableId` con IDs hash de `ParamIdRegistry`, conviene imprimir una vez los IDs que usará el runtime. `ParamIdRegistry` genera IDs estables por string y preregistra varios nombres comunes, incluyendo `gain` y `gate`; después del parche debería incluir también `pitch`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Haz esto una sola vez, por ejemplo al cargar preset:

</span></span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" gate="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> reg</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">getStableId</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"gate"</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span></span><span class="token token">" cutoff="</span><span></span><span class="token token operator"><<</span><span> reg</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">getStableId</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">"cutoff"</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

## Qué esperaría ver

El patrón sano sería:

* FIRE genera Note On/Off;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `compileMatrix()` crea 3 rutas;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `modRoutes=3`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `MCV gate` alterna 0/1, `velocity` cambia, `pitch` cambia con la nota;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `gateSignal` en ADSR se mueve;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `pitchCv` en OSC no se queda a cero;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `gainCv` en VCA responde a velocity.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Si quieres, el siguiente paso ya no sería conceptual: te preparo una **tabla de diagnóstico “si ves X en logs, el bug está en Y”** para usarla mientras parcheas.




Sí. Aquí tienes la tabla práctica de diagnóstico, centrada en  **qué ves en logs -> dónde está el fallo -> qué tocar** . El bundle deja bastante claro que la cadena real es `UI trigger -> mUiMidiQueue -> processBlock -> MidiToCvState -> modSignals[60..62] -> modRoutes -> EngineVoiceRuntime`, y que los targets finales del runtime se resuelven contra `ParamIdRegistry("pitch"|"gate"|"gain")`. [](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Los 3 bugs más probables

Por lo que se ve en el bundle, yo priorizaría estos tres:

1. **Mismatch de IDs de target.** `RuntimeModRoute.targetParamId` está documentado como ID estable de `ParamIdRegistry`, pero `compileMatrix()` está metiendo `static_cast<uint32_t>(targetId)` desde `ModulationRegistry`, lo que huele muchísimo a incompatibilidad semántico/runtime.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
2. **`pitch` no preregistrado o no homologado.** `ParamIdRegistry::preRegisterCommonIds()` incluye `gain` y `gate`, pero en el bundle no aparece `pitch` entre los preregistros visibles, mientras que `EngineVoiceRuntime` sí consulta `getStableId("pitch")`. Eso puede funcionar por hashing dinámico, pero también delata fragilidad contractual.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
3. **Contrato de pitch en unidades equivocadas.** `MidiToCvState` publica `lastPitchHz`, y luego el runtime hace `state.frequencyHz + pitchCv`. Si `state.frequencyHz` ya es frecuencia absoluta y `pitchCv` también está en Hz absolutos, estás sumando dos frecuencias absolutas, no una frecuencia base más una desviación musical.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Cómo leer rápido los resultados

Usa esta regla:

* Si falla **antes** de `[MCV]`, el problema es de entrada MIDI/UI.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* Si `[MCV]` está bien pero fallan `[ROUTES]`, el problema es de matrix/compiler.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* Si `[ROUTES]` está bien pero fallan `[ADSR]/[OSC]/[VCA]`, el problema es de runtime IDs o units.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* Si todo eso está bien y musicalmente sigue mal, el problema es de escala/contrato DSP.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Qué tocar primero

Yo iría en este orden:

1. comprobar que `route.targetParamId` usa exactamente el mismo sistema que `ParamIdRegistry`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
2. asegurar que `pitch`, `gate` y `gain` están todos normalizados en el mismo registry;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
3. revisar si `mcv pitch` debe ser Hz absoluto, offset en Hz o semitonos;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
4. después ajustar amount/escalas.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

La **sospecha principal** sigue siendo la primera: la matrix parece compilar targets semánticos a un enum/ID propio, mientras que el runtime compara contra hashes de `ParamIdRegistry`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Si quieres, en el siguiente mensaje te puedo escribir el **parche exacto que yo haría en `ModulationMatrixService::compileMatrix()`** para unificar IDs y dejar cerrada esa parte.




Sí: con lo que aparece en el bundle, el parche correcto en `ModulationMatrixService::compileMatrix()` es **dejar de escribir `TargetStableId` directamente en `route.targetParamId`** y traducir siempre el target semántico al ID real de `ParamIdRegistry`, porque `RuntimeModRoute.targetParamIdSí: aquí va el parche que yo haría primero. El problema más claro del bundle es que<span> </span>`RuntimeModRoute.targetParamId `<span> </span>está definido como ID estable de<span> </span>`ParamIdRegistry `, pero<span> </span>`ModulationMatrixService::compileMatrix()`<span> </span>está metiendo<span> </span>`static_cast<uint32_t>(TargetStableId)`<span> </span>de<span> </span>`ModulationRegistry `, mientras que<span> </span>`EngineVoiceRuntime `<span> </span>compara luego contra<span> </span>`ParamIdRegistry::getStableId("pitch"|"gate"|"gain")`; eso deja a la matrix y al runtime hablando dos idiomas distintos. [file:2]

## Parche principal

La corrección es simple en concepto: `ModulationRegistry` puede seguir siendo la capa semántica para validar targets, pero **antes** de escribir `route.targetParamId` hay que traducir ese target semántico al `parameterId` real del DSP, que en tu metadata ya existe como `TargetDescriptor.parameterId`. `getAvailableTargets()` ya declara esa correspondencia: por ejemplo `layer.a.osc.pitch -> "pitch"` y `layer.a.vca.gain -> "gain"`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Ese cambio alinea por fin la semántica UI/preset con los IDs reales que consume el audio thread.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Alias necesarios

El default preset actual usa `mcv.1.pitch`, `mcv.1.gate`, `mcv.1.vel` como sources y `A.pitch`, `A.gate`, `A.gain` como targets. Pero la implementación vieja de `ModulationRegistry` que aparece en el bundle solo conoce fuentes tipo `lfo.1`, `env.1`, `midi.vel`, `midi.mw` y targets tipo `layer.a.osc.pitch`, `layer.a.vca.gain`, etc.; por tanto, aunque arregles los IDs, **seguirás descartando slots** si no añades esos aliases.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Yo ampliaría `ModulationRegistry` así:

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span></span><span class="token token">"layer.a.vcf.res"</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> TargetStableId</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span>Resonance </span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span>

</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// aliases semánticos nuevos</span><span>

Eso hace que los defaults actuales del preset dejen de caerse silenciosamente durante compilación.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Añadir `pitch` y `gate`

El runtime consulta `ParamIdRegistry::getStableId("pitch")` y `getStableId("gate")`, mientras que `RuntimeModRoute.targetParamId` se documenta como perteneciente a ese mismo registry. En el bundle `preRegisterCommonIds()` ya contiene `gain` y `gate`, pero no se ve `pitch`, así que yo lo añadiría explícitamente para eliminar ambigüedad y asegurar determinismo.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Soporte a `A.gate`

`getAvailableTargets()` viejo solo expone targets con `parameterId` reales como `"pitch"`, `"cutoff"`, `"resonance"`, `"gain"`, `"pwm"`, etc. No aparece un target estándar de gate allí, aunque `EngineVoiceRuntime` sí tiene tratamiento especial con `getModSignal(idGate, ...)`. Eso significa que `A.gate` no es un parámetro “normal”, sino una señal de control especial; si quieres mantener ese naming en presets/UI, debes resolverlo manualmente a `"gate"` como hice arriba.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Así la capa semántica y la UI ya no dependen de hacks externos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Lo que espero tras el parche

Después de este cambio deberías ver:

* que `compileMatrix()` ya no descarta los 3 slots por source/target inválidos;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* que `modRouteCount` pasa a 3 o más en el preset por defecto;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* que `A.gate` dispara el ADSR porque `getModSignal(idGate, ...)` encontrará una ruta real;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* que `A.gain` modulará el VCA porque `unit.stableParamIds[0]` en el VCA ya usa `ParamIdRegistry("gain")`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y que `A.pitch` por fin llegará al oscilador, aunque ahí todavía puede quedar pendiente el contrato de unidades Hz vs semitonos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## El siguiente problema probable

Una vez arreglado esto, el siguiente bug casi seguro será el de  **pitch en unidades** . Tu MCV publica `lastPitchHz`, pero el runtime hace `freq = state.frequencyHz + pitchCv`; si `pitchCv` es frecuencia absoluta, estás sumando dos absolutos y el resultado es físicamente incorrecto.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

O sea: **primero** arreglaría el mismatch de IDs y aliases; **después** revisaría si `mcv.1.pitch` debe ser:

* semitonos relativos,
* ratio exponencial,
* o frecuencia absoluta con reemplazo, no suma.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Mi apuesta es que con este parche ya recuperarás **gate** y probablemente  **gain** , y dejarás **pitch** al menos visible como siguiente bug real, en vez de oculto detrás del fallo de routing.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

¿Quieres que el siguiente mensaje te lo escriba como  **diff casi listo para pegar** , con los bloques exactos por fichero?




Sí. Te lo dejo ya como  **diff mental casi pegable** , separando qué tocar en cada fichero y por qué. La base del cambio es esta: `ModulationMatrixService` hoy escribe `TargetStableId` en `RuntimeModRoute.targetParamId`, pero ese campo está definido para IDs de `ParamIdRegistry`, y el runtime compara contra `getStableId("pitch"|"gate"|"gain")`, así que hay que traducir semántica -> parámetro runtime real antes de compilar la ruta. [](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 1) `ModulationMatrixService.cpp`

Este es el parche importante. Ahora mismo hace `route.targetParamId = static_cast<uint32_t>(targetId)`, y eso es el origen del desacople con `EngineVoiceRuntime`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Con eso, las rutas compiladas ya hablarán el mismo idioma que el runtime.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 2) `ModulationMetadata.h`

Aquí necesitas dos cosas: aceptar los nombres nuevos del sistema semántico y exponerlos también en metadata. El bundle muestra que el preset default usa `mcv.1.pitch`, `mcv.1.gate`, `mcv.1.vel` y `A.pitch`, `A.gate`, `A.gain`, mientras que el `ModulationRegistry` viejo solo reconoce `midi.vel`, `midi.mw`, `layer.a.osc.pitch`, `layer.a.vca.gain`, etc.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 3) `ParamIdRegistry.h`

Aquí haría un cambio pequeño pero sano: preregistrar `pitch`. El runtime llama a `getStableId("pitch")`, y aunque el registry puede crearlo on demand, mejor dejarlo fijado explícitamente junto a `gate` y `gain`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
