Esto reduce incertidumbre y asegura que el engine arranque con esos IDs definidos al hacer `VirtualAnalogEngine` `preRegisterCommonIds()`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 4) Qué no tocar todavía

Yo  **no tocaría aún** :

* `EngineVoiceRuntime::getModSignal()`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `VoiceArchitectureCompiler`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `VCA` o `ADSR`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* ni el wiring de `mUiMidiQueue`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Todo eso ya parece razonablemente alineado. El bug más fuerte está en la compilación de rutas semánticas hacia IDs runtime.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 5) Resultado esperado tras aplicar esto

Lo esperable es:

* slot 1 `mcv.1.pitch -> A.pitch` ya compila a source `60` y target `ParamIdRegistry("pitch")`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* slot 2 `mcv.1.gate -> A.gate` ya compila a source `61` y target `ParamIdRegistry("gate")`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* slot 3 `mcv.1.vel -> A.gain` ya compila a source `62` y target `ParamIdRegistry("gain")`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `modRouteCount` deja de quedarse a cero o con rutas inútiles;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `gate` y `gain` deberían empezar a funcionar si el resto del chain está vivo.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 6) El bug que probablemente emergerá después

Después de este parche, el siguiente sospechoso será casi seguro el  **dominio de pitch** . `VirtualAnalogEngine` convierte Note On a `pitchHz` y hace `mGlobalMidiToCv.updateFromNoteOn(pitchHz, velocity, ...)`, mientras que `EngineVoiceRuntime` luego hace `freq = state.frequencyHz + pitchCv`; si `pitchCv` es Hz absoluto, la suma no representa una modulación musical correcta.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Eso significa que arreglando el routing probablemente obtendrás:

* gate correcto,[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* velocity/gain visible,[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y pitch “vivo” pero mal escalado o musicalmente incorrecto.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Eso ya sería un **buen** siguiente bug, porque indicaría que el plumbing quedó resuelto.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Orden de aplicación

Yo lo aplicaría así:

1. `ModulationMatrixService.cpp`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
2. `ModulationMetadata.h`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
3. `ParamIdRegistry.h`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
4. recompilar y repetir la prueba mínima con los 3 slots default.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Después de eso, si quieres, te escribo el  **segundo parche** : el de normalizar `pitch` para que no mezcle frecuencia absoluta con offset.




Sí: el segundo parche debe atacar el contrato de  **pitch** , porque en el bundle `MidiToCvState` guarda `lastPitchHz` y `VirtualAnalogEngine` alimenta eso con una frecuencia absoluta calculada desde la nota MIDI, pero `EngineVoiceRuntime` suma luego `pitchCv` sobre `state.frequencyHz`; eso mezcla dos magnitudes absolutas como si una fuera offset.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Qué está mal

Ahora mismo el flujo es básicamente este:

* en `VirtualAnalogEngine`, al recibir `NoteOn`, se calcula `pitchHz = 440 * 2^((note-69)/12)` y se pasa a `mGlobalMidiToCv.updateFromNoteOn(pitchHz, velocity, channel)`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `MidiToCvState` almacena ese valor en `lastPitchHz`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* el bloque `MidiToCv` de `EngineVoiceRuntime` publica esa señal en `modSignals[60]`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y después el oscilador usa algo tipo `freq = state.frequencyHz + pitchCv`.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Si `state.frequencyHz` ya es la frecuencia base de la nota activa, entonces sumar `lastPitchHz` vuelve a añadir otra frecuencia absoluta. Musicalmente eso no representa ni pitch bend, ni key tracking, ni CV 1V/oct, ni offset lineal útil.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Parche recomendado

La corrección más limpia es redefinir la salida `mcv.1.pitch` como  **offset relativo en semitonos** , no como frecuencia absoluta. El runtime ya distingue tipos de señal y tu documentación de modulación dice que `SignalType::Pitch` representa semitonos por bloque, así que esta opción está alineada con tu propio modelo.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 1) Cambiar el estado MIDI-to-CV

En vez de almacenar `lastPitchHz`, yo lo cambiaría por algo como `lastPitchSemitones`, con 0 como valor neutro.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// DSP/Modulation/AceMidiToCv.h</span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> lastVelocity </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">bool</span><span> isGateActive </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">false</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">int</span><span> activeNotes </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">bool</span><span> activityPulse </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">false</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>

</span></span><span><span>        lastVelocity </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        isGateActive </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">false</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        activeNotes </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        activityPulse </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">false</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        midiChannel </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">bool</span><span></span><span class="token token">shouldProcess</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">int</span><span> incomingChannel</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token">const</span><span></span><span class="token token">noexcept</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">return</span><span> midiChannel </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">||</span><span> midiChannel </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span> incomingChannel</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">return</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>        lastPitchSemitones </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> pitchSemitones</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>

</span></span><span><span>        activityPulse </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">true</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">return</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>        activeNotes </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> std</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">max</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> activeNotes </span><span class="token token operator">-</span><span></span><span class="token token">1</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">if</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>activeNotes </span><span class="token token operator">==</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>            isGateActive </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">false</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>        activityPulse </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">true</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">void</span><span></span><span class="token token">updateFromKill</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">{</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        activeNotes </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">0</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        isGateActive </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">false</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        activityPulse </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token boolean">true</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">}</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Este cambio vuelve la salida de pitch coherente con una señal de modulación.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 2) Cambiar la llamada desde `VirtualAnalogEngine`

En el render loop, en vez de pasar `pitchHz` al MIDI-to-CV global, hay que pasar el número de nota o un offset relativo. La solución más directa es pasar el número MIDI como semitonos absolutos y luego convertirlo a offset relativo en el runtime, o mejor aún, pasar ya un offset relativo respecto a la nota base de la voz.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Como primer parche seguro, yo haría esto:

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// en VirtualAnalogEngine, dentro del manejo de NoteOn</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">const</span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> midiNote </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> e</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>data</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>noteOn</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>pitch</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">const</span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> pitchHz </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">440.0f</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">*</span><span> std</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">pow</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">2.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span></span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>midiNote </span><span class="token token operator">-</span><span></span><span class="token token">69.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">/</span><span></span><span class="token token">12.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span>mVoices</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>voiceIdx</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">handleNoteOn</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>e</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>data</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>noteOn</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>pitch</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> pitchHz</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> e</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>data</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>noteOn</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>velocity</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// antes:</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// mGlobalMidiToCv.updateFromNoteOn(pitchHz, e.data.noteOn.velocity, e.channel);</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// después:</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>mGlobalMidiToCv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span class="token token">updateFromNoteOn</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>midiNote</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> e</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>data</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>noteOn</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>velocity</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> e</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>channel</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Así la señal `mcv.1.pitch` deja de ser Hz absoluto y pasa a ser nota MIDI en semitonos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 3) Convertir a offset en `EngineVoiceRuntime`

Luego, cuando el bloque 501 publique su pitch al espacio de modulación, conviene convertirlo a **desviación relativa** respecto a la nota actual de la voz. Como `VoiceState` guarda `noteId` y `frequencyHz`, puedes calcular el número MIDI base a partir de la frecuencia o, mejor, si la voz ya conoce su nota original, usar esa directamente.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

La idea sería algo así:

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// dentro del case 501 (MidiToCv) en EngineVoiceRuntime</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">auto</span><span class="token token operator">&</span><span> mcv </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>units</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span>u</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>data</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// antes:</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// state.modSignals[60] = mcv.lastPitchHz;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// después:</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> baseMidiNote </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span></span><span class="token token">69.0f</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">+</span><span></span><span class="token token">12.0f</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">*</span><span> std</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">log2</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span>std</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">max</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">1.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>frequencyHz</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">/</span><span></span><span class="token token">440.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span class="token token">60</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>lastPitchSemitones </span><span class="token token operator">-</span><span> baseMidiNote</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span class="token token">61</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>isGateActive </span><span class="token token operator">?</span><span></span><span class="token token">1.0f</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">:</span><span></span><span class="token token">0.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>modSignals</span><span class="token token punctuation">[</span><span class="token token">62</span><span class="token token punctuation">]</span><span></span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> mcv</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>lastVelocity</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Eso hace que, para la nota recién pulsada y asignada a la voz, el offset sea aproximadamente 0 semitonos; y si luego reutilizas el bloque para otro evento o para pitch bend futuro, la magnitud sigue siendo musical.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 4) Aplicar el pitch correctamente al oscilador

Si el oscilador estaba haciendo `freq = state.frequencyHz + pitchCv`, deberías cambiarlo a fórmula exponencial, porque el dominio correcto para semitonos es multiplicativo, no aditivo.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

<pre class="not-prose w-full rounded font-mono text-sm font-extralight"><div class="codeWrapper bg-subtle text-light selection:text-super selection:bg-super/10 my-md relative flex flex-col rounded-lg font-mono text-sm font-medium"><div class="translate-y-xs -translate-x-xs bottom-xl mb-xl flex h-0 items-start justify-end sm:sticky sm:top-xs"><div class="overflow-hidden border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-base rounded-full"><div class="border-subtlest ring-subtlest divide-subtlest bg-subtle"></div></div></div><div class="-mt-xl"><div><div data-testid="code-language-indicator" class="text-quiet bg-quiet py-xs px-sm inline-block rounded-br rounded-tl-lg text-xs font-thin">cpp</div></div><div><span><code><span><span class="token token">// antes</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> freq </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>frequencyHz </span><span class="token token operator">+</span><span> pitchCv</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token">// después</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token">float</span><span> freq </span><span class="token token operator">=</span><span> state</span><span class="token token punctuation">.</span><span>frequencyHz </span><span class="token token operator">*</span><span> std</span><span class="token token double-colon punctuation">::</span><span class="token token">pow</span><span class="token token punctuation">(</span><span class="token token">2.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">,</span><span> pitchCv </span><span class="token token operator">/</span><span></span><span class="token token">12.0f</span><span class="token token punctuation">)</span><span class="token token punctuation">;</span></span></code></span></div></div></div></pre>

Ese es el contrato natural para una señal de pitch en semitonos.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## 5) Opción todavía mejor

La versión realmente buena sería:

* `mcv.1.pitch` no representar “nota MIDI absoluta”, sino un  **CV pitch offset** ;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* la nota base de la voz entrar por `state.frequencyHz`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* el bloque MIDI-to-CV emitir `0` en pitch para una nota recién asignada;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y reservar esa salida para bend, glide, microtuning, quantizer, secuenciador o señales transpositoras.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Pero para destrabar el proyecto, el parche anterior ya basta para sacar el pitch del estado roto y llevarlo a una semántica consistente.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Orden práctico

Yo haría el segundo parche en este orden:

1. renombrar `lastPitchHz` -> `lastPitchSemitones`;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
2. cambiar `updateFromNoteOn()` para recibir nota MIDI, no Hz;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
3. publicar en `modSignals[60]` un offset relativo, no frecuencia absoluta;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
4. cambiar el uso del oscilador a multiplicación exponencial.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

## Lo que deberías notar

Tras esto, lo esperable es:

* `gate` ya funcione de forma estable;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `velocity -> gain` ya sea audible;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* `pitch` deje de producir offsets absurdos en Hz;[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)
* y el sistema quede preparado para meter luego bend, glide o MPE sin romper el contrato semántico.[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

El siguiente paso lógico sería revisar si quieres que `mcv.1.pitch` sea:

* nota absoluta,
* offset relativo,
* o dos salidas separadas (`note` y `pitchOffset`).[](https://ppl-ai-file-upload.s3.amazonaws.com/web/direct-files/attachments/20816342/3b03b989-c302-49af-9f04-caf218a06e2e/project_bundle_202604032025-2.jsonl)

Mi recomendación clara es:  **base note fuera de la matrix, pitch offset dentro de la matrix** .
